The two fixtures the loader will need exist as Python (`T_ROLES/T_INST` in `first_face_and_tower.py:29–30`; the triangle in `thin_cast_readings.py`).

### ★★ §97.1 · SURFACE 1 — the premise of my charter was FALSE, and that is the ruling
**MOLD v4 §7's five are NOT refusals — they are PROMISES, in the device's first person, to the caster** (*"what the device promises in return: it will never fill a name, never posit an identification, never assert a relation in your vocabulary that you did not cast, never read an absence as a fact, and never fail a cast for what its neighbours turned out to be"*). Four are not load-time events; only the fifth mentions failing and promises NOT to. ⇒ ***A PROMISE AND A REFUSAL ARE DIFFERENT SPEECH ACTS: a refusal is an EVENT with a reason; a promise is STANDING and kept by conduct.*** **All five are discharged by BEHAVIOUR, each with a visible form:** the card shows `label` and the cast's own name as two registers, never merged · the edge stays given-less until given · the card reads `relations` and nothing else · unlisted tuples read UNRECORDED, never *does-not-hold* · **load state is PER CORNER — A stays taken when B fails.** ⛔ ***A promise that must be STATED is a promise the surface does not keep VISIBLY*** — reciting them marks the ordinary. **They are not copy; they are the ACCEPTANCE TEST for the surface.**
★★★ **A CLEAN LOAD IS NOT A CLEAN CAST — THE DEVICE NEVER GRADES.** If a load succeeded quietly the person would infer approval — *silence as a positive mark for a fact the device does not have.* No tick, no *valid*, no *ok*, no green. **The card's first line states what it COUNTED and what it carried UNREAD: `taken — N roles · M relation-types · K relations · A axioms carried, never evaluated · warrant carried, never read`.** *`carried, never evaluated` is the one permanent caveat and it is the type's own word.* ⇒ **Under Arman's range point (below) this stops being a nicety and becomes THE load-bearing rule: a device that grades cannot accept many shapes, because grading requires one standard.**
**THE GESTURE:** `load cast… (.cast.json)` inside `EDIT SELECTED PACKET` beside `Save packet` — the extension in the label is the module's own idiom (`load universe… (.snapshot.json)`). ⛔ **Two self-corrections from grounding, both hers:** the GRAIN needs no words — the section's heading IS `EDIT SELECTED PACKET`, so *position carries the link*; and **by her own rule correctly applied the button would be OUT of the line** (grain by position, refusals by behaviour — the residue empty) — *"the rule was right; my application was from a diagram, not the app."* ⇒ **IN for a different, measured reason: DISCOVERABILITY — the button is five acts deep (`workspace → cell → selection → vertex → packets`), the edge rows' argument exactly** ⇒ the clause **`a corner takes a concept-space from the packets tab`**. ⚠ **THE `Custom JSON` HAZARD, named now because it is cheap now:** the editor already has a free-form JSON field into `custom`; a person with a cast in hand will reasonably paste it there — into the blob, not the typed `cast?` — **the two must not read as siblings** (load a FILE vs paste TEXT is the mark, only if the surface does not place them as two ways to do one thing).

### ⛔ §97.2 · ARMAN'S FIRST CATCH — the checks were designed against a DOCUMENT, not the SUBSTRATE
Her register 2 (*`8 of 49 relations carry no reason` · `type declared on 9 of 14 roles`*) checked **fields the type does not have** — the coder had dropped every practice-specific field of MOLD v4 §5 deliberately. **Withdrawn by her own hand within sixteen minutes of Arman's catch; her first law broken and owned: SUBSTRATE BEFORE RULING.** ★ **And his point was larger than her error — verbatim: *"the casts will be in multiple shapes — one might be like this run's, but there might be casts like simple diagrams (a concept-space expressed as a triangulation itself, three instances with three relations), and many other forms."*** ⇒ ⛔ **MOLD v4 IS ONE PROFILE over the type — the hygiene of one research practice — and she had been building the device's default reading of EVERY cast out of it: *the device scolding a good cast for not being a literature review* — breaking, at scale, the very promise (*never fail a cast for its neighbours*) she had spent the same letter defending.** ⇒ **HER REPLACEMENT RULE: *the device checks what is true of a STRUCTURE, never what is true of a PRACTICE.*** ⚠ She BLOCKED surface 2 ON PURPOSE (*"design for the FILE (MOLD v4 §5)" in my charter would have baked the profile into the UI*) and routed the range to the researcher as a definition — cost: one letter, one hour, nothing built. *That is the circuit working: the designer refuses to draw what she would have to undo; the researcher rules the definition; Arman catches the drift inside the hour.* ⛔ **MY CHARTER'S "design for the FILE (MOLD v4 §5)" was the same error one seat up — the profile named as the schema. Corrected here: the schema is the TYPE; MOLD v4 §5 is one file-profile the loader may READ, mapping to the type and carrying the rest unread.**

### ★★ §97.3 · THE RANGE OF A CAST — ruled in 46 minutes, corrected in 29 more on Arman's second catch
**RANGE = `ConceptSpace`, because the type carries the RULED kind** (P0: a finite relational structure, relations given, lattice derived; 0031 §1.1) — *not because the type is all we could think of.* "Many shapes" are shapes of relational structure; what is outside (infinite instance-spaces, amounts as structure, relations between relations except by reification) is outside by the sovereign ruling. **A THIN CAST IS FIRST-CLASS** — well-formedness and no more; the device may never grade thinness, it must READ it (Arman's triangle: `|Aut| = 3` directed / 6 undirected / 1 with three relation-types; tied candidates and their meet accordingly; the empty core as a state). ***A cast of nothing ≠ no cast.*** **τ ranges over unary types too** (the type gives arity-1 relations two homes; the consumer treats them as one). ★ **TERM ORDER IS CONTENT** — an ordered face tuple breaks the triangle's rotation (`|Aut|` 3 → 1, computed; *the researcher's own seal said otherwise and fired*): a symmetric relation is cast extensionally or read as DIRECTED — D14 at the cast grain. ⛔ **THE STRUCTURAL CHECKS ARE THREE — CLOSURE · ARITY · CONSISTENCY** (the 1319 correction, on Arman's catch: the researcher had answered the designer's *relation-type absent from the signature* with *role types declared or UNKNOWN* — a PROFILE clause, mis-filed as structural — and OMITTED a check): **closure** = every reference resolves (terms to roles; relation types to the signature — an unresolved type has no arity, so arity presupposes closure) · **arity** · **consistency** = *the record is a FUNCTION*: no tuple with both polarities, no duplicate role id, no signature name with two arities — **the type as cut can REPRESENT the contradiction, so the device must refuse it BY NAME.** "Declared or UNKNOWN" moves to the profile; an omitted unary type reads as unrecorded.
⚠ **A CARRIED-CONTENT GAP, named by the researcher for pricing:** the type has no field for the caster's SUBJECT MATTER — *a concept = a structure together with what it is OF*, the mold's stipulation, load-bearing for concept identity across isomorphic casts. It rides in `warrant` or nowhere; **never the corner's `label`.** ⇒ **A typed field is right (subject matter is READ, beside the label — `warrant` is carried unread); it is a SECOND spend on `geometry.ts` (frozen) — priced by the coder inside `C-6c`, sanctioned by Arman, landed alone before the loader maps a file that carries it.**

### ★★ §97.4 · SURFACE 2 — the corner's card reads a held cast; TWO refusals, not one
**THREE STATES, not two:** no cast (`cast` undefined) → **NO ROW — a true absence** · **a cast of nothing** (`roles: []`) → `a cast of nothing — no roles` — *a cast IS held; loading it was a positive act* · a cast with content → the counts. `n-a` not re-minted. **THE COUNTS — ONE number across both homes of τ** (the researcher's clarification found a FALSE COUNT one letter from shipping: `relations.length` undercounts every unary relation cast in `roles[].types` — *the two homes are an artefact of the TYPE, never content of the CAST; the card must never expose an implementation split as something the person did*). ★★ **THE TERM-ORDER TRAP — the highest-value line on the card and the ONLY place it can be caught:** a person casts `r(A,B) · r(B,C) · r(C,A)` meaning undirected; the device holds a DIRECTED triangle; the type has no `symmetric` field and needs none — **nothing in the record can warn them; the surface is the only place it is visible.** ⇒ **each relation-type shows its listed orderings as a COUNT: `r · 3 tuples · none reversed` · `r · 6 tuples · every one reversed` · `r · 5 tuples · 2 reversed`** — no verdict, no *you probably meant symmetric* (POSITING), no error; the person who meant symmetric sees `none reversed` and knows. **TWO REGISTERS, never merged — and thinness makes the UNLABELLED role the ORDINARY case:** a role with no `label` shows its `id` AS AN ADDRESS, marked as one, never as a name (the name-slot law at the caster's grain, firing daily). **`UNKNOWN` shown where it was written + one count; OMISSION SILENT** (*writing UNKNOWN is a positive act — "I looked and I do not know"; omitted is the ordinary case under thin casts — do not mark it*).
⛔ **TWO REFUSALS, not one (her 1324, revising her own 1227 on the researcher's correction):** **(1) NOT A CAST** — unparseable, or no `roles`/`signature`/`relations` — `this file is not a cast`, blunt because there is nothing to point at. **(2) A CONTRADICTORY RECORD — parses perfectly and MUST STILL BE REFUSED:** closure and arity breaks are LOCAL (take and mark — *refusing a file over one bad entry would ERASE the rest*); **a contradiction is NOT local, because the device must give that tuple a status in every downstream reading and every status available is FORBIDDEN** — count it either way = PICKING a polarity; treat it as unrecorded = converting a stated contradiction into a non-statement = ERASING, *reading an absence as a fact run backwards*. ⇒ ***There is no reading of a contradictory tuple the device is permitted to make, so it must not take it — not out of strictness, but because taking it would require an act it has promised never to perform.*** **Refused at the CAST, not the tuple** (a partially-taken cast would leave the corner holding something the person did not write, silently — her grounds; ⚠ the scope routed to the researcher as one line, not assumed). ★ **And it is the GOOD refusal — the device read it, understood it, and can point at the line: `not taken — the record states two things about one tuple · r(A, B) is listed both holds and does-not-hold`** (siblings: `role id "A" is declared twice` · `type "r" is declared with arity 2 and arity 3`). *The case that looked like a stricter rule is the case where the device is finally USEFUL when it says no.*
**What is surface 3's and NOT spent early (hers, correctly):** `|Aut|`, tied candidates, the meet, exposure, the EMPTY CORE as a state — all about identification ACROSS an edge. The orderings count is the one exception: a fact about ONE cast.

### §107.1 · THE DRIFT — real, and this office's
From Δ72's *"an edge is the AMALGAM over a GIVEN J"* to `C-6d (β)/(γ)`, "given" became "chosen from a list the device computed": the researcher pinned candidates and weights, the designer designed an offering, I ratified and chartered it — and the question *should the app compute the candidates at all?* was never put to Arman. The offering treated agreement counts between two provisional records as reasons to identify, and ranked by them. ⇒ **the device manufacturing a reason it does not have** — the honesty catch, his §4.1, judged RIGHT. Second fault, the midwife's: the identifications were computed from the two PARENTS alone — the parent-blend failure — with the opposite vertex absent. Third: the register sat at a g0 edge with no consumer — a mark without a route (law 7), which is what he felt as "how is the mapping doable without the midpoint."

### §107.2 · THE HONESTY LINE — ruled for every surface of the layer from here
The device may honestly say four things: **the FORM** (what any mapping must be — a function, one-to-one, and the glued record a structure: no contradiction) · **the RECORD** (what each cast says, shown whole — the inside of a space, the opposite vertices' spaces beside the parents) · **the REFUSAL** (a pair that makes the glued record say two things about one tuple, refused BY NAME — refutation is structural) · **the TRACE** (after the person has mapped: what the act did to the record — what now sits on one role; what each parent still holds that the child does not, the midwife's residuals — as a description, never a score). **It may not say which role is which, which pairings are better, or anything that treats agreement as evidence of sameness — including "these relate to `C` the same way, so consider them."** ⇒ **the device's same-name TYPE PROPOSAL (§105.1, built at `6b642bb`) falls under the same catch**: that a name occurs in both files is a fact the device may show; that it is the same relation is the person's to say. The mold's own types (`member_status`) stay shared by definition — the device's contract, not a caster's word.

### §109.1 · The five discoveries, ranked by what they change
1. **THE HONESTY LINE WAS RULED IN JUNE — `CAL-2` (sovereign, 2026-06-14): *"FRAME + QUESTIONS ONLY. The system fills the fixed frame … and poses the questions; the HUMAN does all Ω/Λ/X reasoning. 2. **THE GROUND PLAN (`docs/PLATONIC_ENGINE_GROUND_PLAN.md`) — the project's orientation layer, written *"to prevent drift"* — carries the atomic principle verbatim:** *"A midpoint can be mediation of its parent edge and also a projection/sublimation site of the face-local opposite vertex or vertices."* And three more rules this office re-derived this week: §4.5 *"Topological co-location is not semantic identity … the topology may say 'one support,' but semantics must still say whether the names are co-located, identified, fused, constrained, conflicted, or rejected"* (= the unmapped midpoint is the disjoint union, §108.1.2); §5.1 *"Model before UI … pure types → pure computation → diagnostic output → small read-only UI"*; §5.5 *"Return unsupported often … a clean unsupported state is better than a false semantic reading"*; §5.6 *"Do not auto-name too early."* §3.4's caution — *do not silently generalize the packet editor to … edges* — was not breached (Δ75 sanctioned the edge datum), but it was never cited.

### §110.2 · THE DESIGNER'S `C-7a` / `C-7b` — RATIFIED whole; three things she did that this office should copy
- **She took the researcher's correction of her own §4 whole and named what she had collapsed:** *the midwife's `X` is a NAME reached by contraction; the layer's `M` is a SPACE reached by union; they meet at the core.* The anti-blend device survives in FORM (a subtraction on the line, never a bare line); only the subtrahend was wrong (`A ⊖ K`, not `A ⊖ M`).
- ⛔ **She refused the obvious form with a reason from the ground, not taste: NOT A RING.** Flow is 14 points and 31 arrows; on a circle those are 31 chords and *the eye reads CROSSINGS as structure*. ⇒ ★ ***a layout may add no meaning; a ring adds meaning that is false*** — the researcher's *positions carry nothing* turned into a design verdict.
- ★★★ **And the question she refused to guess two hours earlier answered itself from the form: A COLUMN IS ALREADY A LIST** — 14 points is 14 rows; it degrades by scrolling, which every other surface here does; *a ring cannot degrade, only shrink until unreadable.* She had said she wanted the researcher's §1 first and would not draw without it; she got it, and the constraint (faithfulness only) made the choice decidable rather than a taste call. **That is the discipline the office wants: hold the ruling until its ground arrives, then rule from the ground.**
**`C-7a` — THE INSIDE = an ARC DIAGRAM over the caster's own order:** points in a COLUMN in the caster's order (the `label` if given, else the id AS AN ADDRESS); unary/`member_status` a BADGE on the point, value in words, with polarity; arity-2 distinct an ARC beside the column with its word; `t₁ = t₂` a LOOP at the point (Φ1 six, Flow three); arity ≥ 3 a TUPLE-NODE offset from the column with NUMBERED legs; `does-not-hold` a distinct glyph on the arc (a VALUE — T's three negatives are three drawn arcs); **the unrecorded NOTHING**; axioms/warrant text beside. ★ **DIRECTION CARRIED BY SIDE, free and honest — the side is a FUNCTION OF THE TUPLE, not a choice** (her *term order is content*, paid out as geometry). The two absences: **no cast → NOTHING, no panel**; **a cast of nothing → `a cast of nothing — no roles`, the card's own sentence** — never an empty column, which would read as *not loaded*. ⚠ Unruled by her, correctly: which side means "down the caster's order" — a first-run-at-the-eye call.
**`C-7b` — THE MIDPOINT'S GESTURES:** pointing = click a point in `A`'s column, then one in `B`'s; the pair drawn as a LINE ACROSS THE FOLD, marked `yours`, with `withdraw` (the landed register idiom kept — one vocabulary for *the act was mine*); words the same way between the two word-lists; **no hint of any kind — no same-name highlight, no similarity sort, no proximity; the caster's order on both sides.** **THE REFUSAL AT THE ACT, where the pair was made, every offending tuple with BOTH values and BOTH parents — and it must OFFER both withdrawals: ★ *naming a conflict while allowing only one undo would decide for the person which half was wrong*** (a two-handed control, not an error message). **THE ORIGIN COLOURING — only `both` gets a glyph:** in the unfolded layout *from A* and *from B* are stated BY POSITION, and `both` is the one position cannot state and also the rare one (Flow × Φ: 3 of 24 against 7 and 14) — *marking all three would mark the ordinary twice over.* **THE UNGLUED STATE:** two columns apart, no lines, both vocabularies foreign — and it must not read as *the device found nothing*; the five-state discipline intact (*no pair given yet* ≠ *nothing identified*). A midpoint whose parents do not both hold casts: **NO SURFACE**.
**§3 — her law biting her own layout, twice:** (i) the risk that dissolves, recorded so nobody builds it — two projection vertices must NOT yield two traces (ONE map, ONE core, ONE trace; the unfolding is two faces, not two acts) — **this is the fence Δ81 needs and she wrote it before Arman's word arrived**; (ii) the real bite — **`ρ_A` and `ρ_B` ARE two numbers side by side**, two halves of one act's trace, *"but a person reading `Flow: 11 roles met no partner` beside `Φ: 3` will read *Flow did worse*. A description becomes a comparison by juxtaposition, and the device will not have said a word."* ⇒ **CURE RATIFIED: each residual carries ITS OWN PARENT'S DENOMINATOR — `Flow: 11 of its 14 roles met no partner` · `Φ: 3 of its 9`** — measured against different wholes, the comparison disarmed by the form.
★★ **And she scoped her own ratified ban rather than making a quiet exception: *the ban was never about fractions; it was about a fraction concealing a third value. Where the question is two-valued — a role either met a partner or it did not — the denominator genuinely is the whole and a fraction is honest.*** Ratified as the ban's SCOPE (§98.2's *two counts never a ratio* stands where a third value exists — offers, types, unrecorded). ⇒ **the denominator rule rides with the trace wherever it is built.**

PACKET(site) = { geometry/ambo : genealogy (parents, generation, operation), location, local triad (J; A, B), adjacency/relations

CD → complement {A,B}` · `host = unique edge-match … (6/6)` · gen-2: `6 sites at depth 1 · 12 at depth 2`, 18 packets · `every namingDecision === null`.** ⇒ **Δ81's *both* is not a new fact to build — it is what the engine's own reader computes, and has computed since July.** Marker sent to the coder before it builds: **consume the TRACE; never re-derive parents/host/opposite vertices; do NOT render or extend the FACE** (the naming-era surface, with its `howToName` and blank `Name: ______` — a child that gets a NAME, which Δ71 superseded).

### §111.3 · TWO THINGS THE JUNE MAP OWNS THAT OUR PLAN DOES NOT YET
1. ⭑ **THE ORDERING SPINE (`M5c`), load-bearing and unstated in our plan:** *"a named site's label fills its neighbours' prompts (primals → children → next generation) … **A site's prompt is answerable only once its J/A/B neighbours are named.**"* ⇒ **In our terms: a midpoint's surface is answerable only once its parents hold casts — and at gen ≥ 2 a midpoint's parents are themselves midpoints, so the ambo's generations impose an ORDER on the person's acts.** We have been building gen-1 only. **To the plan as Station C's own dependency; `C-7b` need not solve it, but the plan must not pretend it is absent.**
2. ⭑ **AN OPEN ACCEPTANCE TEST, declined once, still open (`M3F` = the old `D4`, Gate F):** *"THE HUMAN-FRUIT TEST: does the excavation prompt help the human name better than geometry alone?"* ⇒ **the naming layer's own person-acceptance, and it is not the same as Δ67's "each acceptance a person's own sentence": ours asks *did the surface state the act truthfully*; M3F asks *did it help*.** Filed as an open question of the record, not chartered — and worth putting to Arman when `C-7b` is at his hand, because his walk is the only instrument for it.
3. `M7` **THE BRIDGE** — *"answered packets … selectable & liftable as a named subcomplex → the topological module"*, OPEN-DESIGN, mechanism not in repo. **That is our plan's step 2 ("the lift carries") under its older name.** The two plans meet there.

### §111.4 · ⛔ A COLLISION THE LIFT WILL HIT — `CONTEXT.md`'s translation table against the concept-space
`docs/CONTEXT.md` (the topological module's ratified glossary, redrafted 2026-06-23) fixes the reading table: **`argument = a form` · `proposition = a VERTEX (0-cell)` · `inference = an EDGE (1-cell)` · contradiction = an orientation-reversing identification · a contradictory constellation = a signed loop.** ⇒ **In the playground a vertex IS a proposition. In the ambo/concept layer a vertex CARRIES a concept-space whose ROLES are its points.** Both stand — they are different modules — **but our plan's step 2 lifts casts from the ambo into the playground, and at that moment a vertex that was a concept-space becomes a proposition.** Either the cast's roles collapse into one proposition (and the lift loses the space), or the lift refines the playground's grain (and the table needs a line). ⚠ **Routed to the researcher as the LIFT's definitional question, alongside the 0030 §2 rider — not now, but named before we get there rather than discovered in a build.**
- Two more of `CONTEXT.md`'s laws worth holding, both ours already in other words: *"Lineage: **carried, not minted** — a born form inherits its parents' lineage (unlike an ambo midpoint, which **mints** a fresh one)"* (the amalgam is the concept-space analogue of the inherited lineage, so the midpoint mints geometrically and inherits semantically — say that plainly when `C-7b` reports); and **THE WALL**: *"meaning-words … live on the reading side and never enter an operation's definition"* — the ancestor of the honesty line, on the operations side rather than the device's.

### §111.5 · THE GROUND PLAN's rubric — adopted as this office's charter checklist
`§7` (layer clarity · mutation discipline · cost discipline · **semantic honesty**: *"Does it preserve the difference between co-location and identity? Does it avoid collapsing projection-source, parent, counterpart, and semantic name into one thing?"* · reviewability) and `§11`'s forbidden shortcuts (*do not implement multiple major layers in one pass · do not persist heavy or unstable derived data · **do not rely on README as the source of current project truth***). ⇒ **Adopted: every stamp I write from here answers §7's five before it is sent.** §6.2's first-acceptable-form for the atomic layer is, verbatim, the surface we chartered as `C-7b`: *"a pure local-registry report for generated midpoint contexts, showing parents, source edge, incident faces, projection-sources, and candidate atomic reading where supported"* — with its deferrals (no persistent ledger, no recursive workbench, no global atomic selection, no automatic naming) all honoured.
⚠ **The field appendix §15 settles Δ81 independently of `CAL-1`:** *"Atomic layer may read it as **`AB | C` or `AB | (C,D)`**"* — the ground plan's own notation admits both readings, so Arman's *both* is not a reversal of the project's grain, only of one June fork.

⇒ **Law, and this is the last of the week's three:** *the office that holds the helm reads the orientation layer BEFORE it charters, not after a build is retired.* The graph is the index; `GROUND_PLAN` §7 is the checklist; `CONTEXT.md` and the field appendix are the two walls. **All three were in the repo the whole time.**

### §112.2 · THREE OF MY PREMISES FALSIFIED BY MEASUREMENT — all three mine, none the coder's

### §112.3 · ⛔ ONE REAL CONTRADICTION — where the identification LIVES (mine to rule; one measurement chartered)
**Measured by the coder:** a midpoint's `createdBy.sourceEdgeId` names the **PARENT shape's** edge; the ambo re-derives the current shape's edges with fresh ids and no `sourceEdgeId`, so that id resolves nowhere in the shape the person is in — and a `J` given on the seed's seam **before** the ambo does not ride into the ambo shape (a cast on a corner does). The midpoint therefore reads and writes the **current shape's edge between the two parents** (the parent-cell-face edge).
⇒ **RULED (meaning): the record's home is the edge of the shape the person ACTED IN.** ADR 0031 §5 says *on an edge, `J`* — and after Δ80 the act happens at the midpoint, which exists only after the ambo; so the current shape's parent-cell-face edge is the right home and `sourceEdgeId` is not it. **No ambo carry is chartered on that ground.** ⚠ **But a second question falls out that I will NOT rule blind, and it is a possible DATA-LOSS defect: does a second dissection preserve identifications on edges it does not touch?** If the ambo re-derives the whole shape's edges with fresh ids, then a person who glues six midpoints at gen-1 and dissects again **loses every act**. ⇒ **CHARTERED as a MEASUREMENT (`C-7c` item 1): give one `J` at a gen-1 midpoint, dissect again, and report whether the record survives — with the id mechanism named.** If it does not, the ~10 unfrozen lines in `ambo.ts` stop being optional and the price comes to Arman.

**He is right, and the reason is structural, not a build gap.** Δ80 forbids the device computing anything with `C` and `D`; so the surface shows their casts whole; and a picture that nothing depends on IS decoration — *the mark is not the deliverable, the route is* (law 7), arriving now on the projection sources.

### §115.1 · ⛔ THE DATA LOSS IS REAL, ON BOTH ARMS — the mechanism named at the mint, PRICED, not cut
**Measured:** a `J` given at the gen-1 midpoint `AB` (on the record's ruled home, the gen-1 parent-cell-face edge), then `Apply Ambo Dissection` on the core ⇒ the gen-2 edge between A and B is a **different id** carrying **no `identification`**; the same on a residue arm. **Mechanism, at source:** `applyGenericAmboDissection` rebuilds the shape's edges whole through `deriveEdges(faces, shapeId)` (`shape.ts`, FROZEN, read only), which mints `makeEdgeId(shapeId, a, b) = edge:${stableHash(shapeId|pair)}` — **the id depends on the SHAPE id, so the same PAIR gets a fresh id per shape, and the fresh `Edge` carries `id · vertexIds · sourceVertexIds` and nothing else.** ⇒ **a person who glues six midpoints at gen 1 and dissects again keeps every act in the gen-1 shape (still in the store, reachable by selecting it) and sees NONE of them at gen 2.** ★ This is the cheapest-measurement law paying out: chartered as a measurement rather than a cut, and it found the mint instead of a guess.

### §118.5 · THE RESEARCHER'S THREE — RATIFIED; and the third is a gate nobody asked for
1. **`Und` is ONE KIND WITH AN ADDRESS.** `Fix` = the direct map and the route AGREE · `Mov` = they DIVERGE · `Und` = **the circuit BROKE**; divergence is `Mov` by construction, **so no fourth bucket is owed and none may be minted**; every `Und` role has **exactly one first-break edge** (0 of 20,000 failures) ⇒ one glyph, one meaning, one derived address. ⛔⛔ **AND THE WORDING IS RULED AGAINST THE OBVIOUS ONE: `Und` may NEVER be printed as *"the person has not said"* — in 5,860 of 20,000 triples a role is `Und` although the person HAS spoken on both the direct map and the route and they differ; its own forward circuit broke further along. 0031 §1.5's own word is right: *did not return*.** ⚠ **AND THE WHOLE READING IS ORIENTATION-RELATIVE** — the same face walked the other way has a different `Und` set (7,204 of 20,000), since `Und(h⁻¹) = R_A ∖ im h` ⇒ **the surface must STATE THE DIRECTION IT WALKED.** *(This bit us once already — the tower check's medial orientation, 0031 §7.)*
2. **The residue's domain IS the face's core at `A`:** `dom h_A ⊆ dom J_AB` (0 failures) and every role in it was carried by the person around the WHOLE circuit ⇒ `dom h_A` is the face's core; `Und = R_A ⊖ dom h_A` **is §3's `ρ_A = A ⊖ K` one dimension up, differing in exactly one way worth naming: at the edge the core is GIVEN, at the face it is DERIVED.** ⛔ **The ambient set stays `R_A`, never shrunk to the core.** ⇒ **My *"what does `Fix` mean on a role no pair of casts confirmed"* is answered twice: no such role is ever in `Fix` (0 failures), AND THE PREMISE DISSOLVED AT Δ80 — a pair needs no agreement between casts; the pointing is the act.** ⇒ ★ ***`Fix` means the person's direct map and his own route agree about this role — an agreement between two things HE said, never between two casts.***
3. ⭑⭑ **THE THIRD DEFINITION, which neither of us asked for: THE FACE IS REFUSABLE, AND ADR 0031 DOES NOT SAY SO.** §3.2–§3.3 describe the face's colimit as EXISTING and ask only whether it is injective (`Mov = ∅`). **But the face's world identifies `a ∼ h(a)`, and a corner's OWN record can then say two things about one tuple — and then there is no such world.** Same KIND as the edge's refusal; **new SITE, new CAUSE: no single edge can see it; three acts jointly produce it.** **Measured on the record's own casts: over the 42 hand triples of the first face, base T refuses in 6 (7 refusals)** — e.g. `(i)+S1+Q`, where `Mov = {r1 → r8}` merges `r8 ≡ r1` and T's record then says `sustains(r8, r0) = does-not-hold` against `sustains(r1, r0) = holds`. **Base Flow 0 and base Φ 0 — and not by luck: they declare NO negatives and one mark value, so a merge can only ever add `holds`. ⇒ ONLY A CAST THAT DECLARES A NEGATIVE CAN REFUSE A FACE.** ★ **T's declared negatives earn their keep twice: the same `¬sustains(r8, r0)` that refused an EDGE refuses a FACE.** ⇒ ⛔ **THE GUARD: the face's colimit must be ATTEMPTED, never ASSUMED — a build that prints `Fix · Mov · Und` and a cone-deficit without testing the glued record prints a reading of a world that does not exist, and §3.3's cone-deficit PRESUPPOSES the colimit.** The refusal must name **four** things: the two tuples with their values · the corner they belong to · the merged pair that collapsed them · **the three edges whose composition produced the merge** — and the person withdraws one of three acts; the device must not choose. ⚠ **Scope stated by its author: the check is CORNER-LOCAL (sufficient, not necessary) — *"6 of 42" is a LOWER bound.***
4. **ASSOCIATIVITY: the colimit IS associative — 0 differences in 3,000 with the diagram FIXED, against a live control (a mis-induced second step differs 2,256).** With every higher-generation `J` DERIVED (0031 §3.5), both orders are colimits of ONE diagram **and the order of his acts CANNOT matter.** ⇒ **my worry does not arise in that branch.**

**Her 1519 caveat named the failure mode; her very next sentence argued it away:** *"The ORDERING is robust: 1.29 against 13.26 is a tenfold spread, not measurement error."* ⛔ **That sentence WAS the error — and the headline carried the false number to Arman while the caveat stayed in the body.** ⇒ **Her law, filed in her V1 and taken here: *NAMING A FAILURE MODE IS NOT TESTING IT — AND ARGUING IT AWAY WITHOUT THE TEST IS WORSE THAN NOT NAMING IT.*** She corrects the number to Arman herself. ⛔ **AND AGAINST ME: at §117.3 I copied her false sentence into my own ledger as ratified fact (*"the ordering robust (1.29 against 13.26 is tenfold)"*), and at §118.3 I credited her caveat without noticing that the next sentence undid it. I ratified an argument I had not checked and then praised the caveat it defeated.** Two errors of reading, both mine.
- **THE LABEL LANE IS ARITY-1's — binary words leave it.** At the eye: `F3 · has` → `presupposes` → `F4 · has` → the relation word reads as a CAPTION of the role beside it (`F4 · has · presupposes`, a role with two properties). ★★ **And her own ratified law already decides it: *only arity 2 is an arrow; arity 1 is a mark on the node* — the same law applies to WORDS as to marks. `has` belongs in that lane because a unary relation IS a property of the point; a binary relation's word is a property of its ARC.** ⇒ **RULED: binary words attach to their arcs; a binary word may never be typographically or positionally indistinguishable from a point's own arity-1 marks.**
- **THE WIDTH TAKES THE SECOND ROW — and the other two are ruled out by measurement:** *scroll accepted* NO (Φ's entire right side past the edge makes B second-class — against her own *neither side is main*); *a narrower fold* NO (the fold's slack is ≈ 185 px against an overflow of ≈ 593); **a second row for feet past a width YES — the only one that bounds width BY CONSTRUCTION**, and binary words leaving the label lane gives it room. ★ **And she REOPENS HER OWN CONFIRMATION of Φ1's six loop words** — *"a confirmation made without a fact is revised when the fact arrives"* — the six fall under the same wrap rule. - ★★ **THE CARD'S SECOND CAUSE IS REPETITION, NOT WIDTH — and it is her own line, seven times.** At the eye: six orderings each wrapped before its last word — **SIX ORPHANED `directed`s: the device's READING, the very part she ruled it must disclose, is the part the wrap breaks off and leaves alone** — and the summary line above already says `read as directed`: **the reading appears SEVEN times on one card. DO NOT MARK THE ORDINARY, broken by her own ruled line.** Widening would rejoin each `directed` and leave all seven copies; **and it cannot keep up — Φ has 15 relation-types; no width fixes a height that scales with the vocabulary.** ⇒ **RULED: TERM ORDER GROUPS BY READING, NOT BY RELATION-TYPE** — `read as directed — none reversed: sustains 5 · outlasts 1 · individuates 1 · removes 1 · starts 2 · answers-to 1` / `read as symmetric — all reversed: …` / `read as directed — partly reversed: opposes 5 (2 reversed)` — **at most THREE rows whatever the cast's size; the height bounded by the number of READINGS, not WORDS.** ★ ***A sentence broken before its verdict orphans the verdict; a list broken between items loses nothing.*** The device still discloses its reading — **once per reading instead of once per word.** ⚠ Her *"one cause"* falsified and owned (*"one arithmetic step skipped"*); the column's width stays; the queued plate beside it untouched.

### §122.3 · ⛔ THE DEFECT PAST THE SEAL — the CORNER edge carries by CONTENT, and content cannot tell a doubled seed's two homes apart
**The ratified meaning (my 1110 MARKER; 0028's split):** a CORNER edge CARRIES — the parent's injection into its own born vertex, *derived, total, no record read*; **Arman's stations theorem: the station on a corner edge IS the born vertex's own space.** **The code** (`spaceOf.ts`: `const composed = kind === 'seed' ? emptyComposed() : composedOn(U, V)`) computes the corner edge's `J` with the SAME content meet as a medial edge. **They coincide exactly while the born end holds no seed twice** — i.e. through every station of gen ≤ 3. **At a station over a DOUBLED vertex they part:** a parent's role whose seed the born vertex holds twice finds TWO targets, is left out as a "conflict", and the station houses it a second time.
**MEASURED at my hand** (`_PROBES_122/station_over_doubling.cjs` — the witness's own tower, copied, plus ONE edge: the gen-4 station `S4` on the corner edge `P_A–Q`, `P_A` being `Q`'s own parent; `rngOf(251)`, 3,000 faces): **the gen-2 station = `M_AB`'s own space in 3,000 of 3,000** (the behaviour-neutral half) · `Q` doubles in 2,918 · **the gen-4 station = `Q`'s own space in only 82 of 3,000 — the corner edge's meet leaves conflicts in exactly the 2,918 where `Q` doubles** · **the CONTROL — the same station glued over the pushout's OWN coprojection (the glue's `r.a → r.key`): 3,000 of 3,000.** The first divergent face: `|Q| 15 · |S4| 21` — 6 of `P_A`'s 11 roles housed a second time (the meet paired 5), so a seed `Q` holds twice the station holds three times.
**Where the model stood:** the researcher's `space_of_resolver.py` glues the station over `M_AB["inj"]["A"]` — **the STRUCTURAL injection. The model was right; the build reproduced its gen-2 numbers by a DIFFERENT MECHANISM, and the agreement hid the divergence** (*agreement is not correctness, and it is the fakeable one*). The witness pinned the station at gen 2 alone — ***a correct measurement read as a FENCE at one generation***. And the resolver's head comment says *"the injection is total on the parent and onto its image"* — **a comment stating its precondition: a guard that was never written.**
**Reach:** a gen-4 station needs four dissections with the corner cell at a gen-3 vertex's parent dissected last — **walked by nobody, on no surface a person has seen.** Nothing reachable at gen ≤ 3 moves. ⇒ **`STAMP C-8b` (the coder's mechanism, no meaning question): on a corner edge the `J` IS the born end's own coprojection of its parent, oriented by the edge; no content read; the witness gains the station over a doubled vertex, with the content meet as the control that must fail there.**
⭑ **The meaning question it exposes — the researcher's, not blocking:** on a gen-4 MEDIAL edge (`Q–Q2`, sharing the parent `P_B`; `_PROBES_122/medial_gen4.cjs`) the MEET still never pools (0 of 3,000) but **the doubling COMPOUNDS: a seed role held by 3 or more roles in 2,983 of 3,000 faces (max 4), where at gen 3 the max is 2.** Lawful (the face's holonomy unrolled again) or not — and does the 09-16 characterisation extend? **Owed before any walk reaches gen 4; not before.**

## §134 — THE CARGO INSIDE A CELL: (A) RULED — THE PERSON CARRIES IT ALONG THE RODS. (researcher's `1518`, ruling §24)
*(2026-09-24 15:19 +0330)*
- **At a door:** the transport acts at ALL of the door face's corners at once; the cargo crosses at whichever corner it sits.
- **Inside a cell:** no rule computable from `{pair, side}` may move the cargo — **the one candidate (the cell's own matching) FABRICATED motion in its own no-motion control: 28 of 28 arrivals moved with every map the identity.** Different in-cell routes differ by exactly the face readings the walk exists to test; filling the route is inventing meaning.
- ★ **RULED (A): the cargo moves inside a cell ONLY when the person moves it, corner to corner along a rod (the edge's `J`); the walk's record gains those steps.** Why not (B) *(it stays at its corner)*: on rooms whose paired faces share no corner — the prism, the cube — (B) carries nothing through any door, and **an acceptance that cannot fail in a room like T³ vs SW is the vacuity the plan's first standing rule forbids**; (A) keeps §23's readings live (K2 1,694 · K3 1,764 · K4 803 · controls 0). **The honesty line decides it too: the device never chooses the route — the person does.** Refused: any rule that picks the in-cell route for him. *(Arman may reverse this in one word; it adds a gesture to his walk.)*
- ⇒ **The designer owes one gesture (how the person steps the cargo along a rod inside a cell, where the walk shows it); then `C-11b` charters.**
